SELECT 0 AS "Kind", u.id AS "Id"
FROM users AS u
WHERE u.id = @userId AND u.role = 2 AND u.account_status = 0
UNION ALL
SELECT 1 AS "Kind", c.id AS "Id"
FROM competitions AS c
WHERE c.access_mode = 1 AND c.owner_id <> @userId AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM competition_collaborators AS c0
    WHERE c.id = c0.competition_id AND c0.user_id = @userId) AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM users AS u0
    WHERE u0.id = @userId AND u0.role = 2 AND u0.account_status = 0)
UNION ALL
SELECT 2 AS "Kind", c1.id AS "Id"
FROM competitions AS c1
WHERE c1.deleted_at IS NULL AND (c1.owner_id = @userId OR EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM competition_collaborators AS c2
    WHERE c1.id = c2.competition_id AND c2.user_id = @userId))
UNION ALL
SELECT 3 AS "Kind", t.competition_id AS "Id"
FROM teams AS t
WHERE t.deleted_at IS NULL AND t.registration_status = 1 AND NOT (t.is_banned) AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM team_members AS t0
    WHERE t.id = t0.team_id AND t0.user_id = @userId)
UNION ALL

FROM teams AS t1
WHERE t1.deleted_at IS NULL AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM team_members AS t2
    WHERE t1.id = t2.team_id AND t2.user_id = @userId)

SELECT n2.id, n2.action_value, n2.actor_user_id, n2.appeal_event_id, n2.automatic, n2.body, n2.challenge_flag_id, n2.challenge_title, n2.code, n2.competition_challenge_id, n2.competition_id, n2.count, n2.direction, n2.entry_id, n2.gameplay_fact_failure_code, n2.gameplay_fact_id, n2.gameplay_fact_result, n2.gameplay_fact_state, n2.hint_id, n2.ip_address, n2.jwt_id, n2.kind, n2.live_solo_match_id, n2.live_solo_media_alert_kind, n2.live_solo_media_session_id, n2.live_solo_screen_state, n2.owner_team_id, n2.payload_expires_at, n2.payload_occurred_at, n2.previous_question_status, n2.previous_state_value, n2.provider_name, n2.question_actor_role, n2.question_status, n2.question_subject, n2.range_from, n2.range_to, n2.reason, n2.related_id, n2.related_type, n2.reply_to_id, n2.runtime_instance_id, n2.runtime_state, n2.sent_at, n2.source_event_key, n2.source_id, n2.source_team_id, n2.source_type, n2.sso_protocol, n2.sso_provider_id, n2.state_value, n2.subject, n2.target_id, n2.target_type, n2.team_id, n2.team_name, n2.thread_root_id, n2.title, n2.user_id, n2.user_lifecycle_action, n2.user_name, n2.value, n3.notification_id, n3.reference_kind, n3.count
FROM (
    SELECT n.id, n.action_value, n.actor_user_id, n.appeal_event_id, n.automatic, n.body, n.challenge_flag_id, n.challenge_title, n.code, n.competition_challenge_id, n.competition_id, n.count, n.direction, n.entry_id, n.gameplay_fact_failure_code, n.gameplay_fact_id, n.gameplay_fact_result, n.gameplay_fact_state, n.hint_id, n.ip_address, n.jwt_id, n.kind, n.live_solo_match_id, n.live_solo_media_alert_kind, n.live_solo_media_session_id, n.live_solo_screen_state, n.owner_team_id, n.payload_expires_at, n.payload_occurred_at, n.previous_question_status, n.previous_state_value, n.provider_name, n.question_actor_role, n.question_status, n.question_subject, n.range_from, n.range_to, n.reason, n.related_id, n.related_type, n.reply_to_id, n.runtime_instance_id, n.runtime_state, n.sent_at, n.source_event_key, n.source_id, n.source_team_id, n.source_type, n.sso_protocol, n.sso_provider_id, n.state_value, n.subject, n.target_id, n.target_type, n.team_id, n.team_name, n.thread_root_id, n.title, n.user_id, n.user_lifecycle_action, n.user_name, n.value
    FROM notifications AS n
    WHERE (n.target_type <> 0 OR n.target_id = @userId) AND (n.kind <> 0 OR n.related_type <> 10 OR n.related_type IS NULL OR n.thread_root_id IS NULL OR n.related_id <> n.thread_root_id OR n.related_id IS NULL OR n.action_value NOT IN (1, 2) OR n.action_value IS NULL OR NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM notifications AS n0
        WHERE n0.id = n.thread_root_id AND n0.kind = 1)) AND (n.kind <> 1 OR NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM notifications AS n1
        WHERE n1.thread_root_id = n.id AND n1.kind = 0 AND n1.related_type = 10 AND n1.related_id = n.id AND n1.action_value = 2)) AND ((n.target_type = 0 AND n.target_id = @userId3) OR (n.target_type = 1 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_CollaboratorCompetitionIds)) OR (n.target_type = 2 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_ParticipantCompetitionIds)) OR (n.target_type = 3 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_MemberTeamIds)) OR (n.source_type = 1 AND n.source_id = @userId7) OR (n.thread_root_id IS NULL AND n.id = ANY (@threadRootIds)) OR (n.thread_root_id IS NOT NULL AND n.thread_root_id = ANY (@threadRootIds))) AND n.kind NOT IN (20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25) AND (n.target_type <> 2 OR (n.kind = 1 AND n.source_type = 1)) AND (n.source_type <> 1 OR n.source_id <> @userId10 OR n.source_id IS NULL) AND n.related_type = 1 AND n.related_id = @id
    ORDER BY n.sent_at DESC, n.id DESC
    LIMIT @p13 OFFSET @p
) AS n2
LEFT JOIN notification_reference_counts AS n3 ON n2.id = n3.notification_id
WHERE n2.kind NOT IN (20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25)
ORDER BY n2.sent_at DESC, n2.id DESC, n3.notification_id

SELECT n2.id, n2.action_value, n2.actor_user_id, n2.appeal_event_id, n2.automatic, n2.body, n2.challenge_flag_id, n2.challenge_title, n2.code, n2.competition_challenge_id, n2.competition_id, n2.count, n2.direction, n2.entry_id, n2.gameplay_fact_failure_code, n2.gameplay_fact_id, n2.gameplay_fact_result, n2.gameplay_fact_state, n2.hint_id, n2.ip_address, n2.jwt_id, n2.kind, n2.live_solo_match_id, n2.live_solo_media_alert_kind, n2.live_solo_media_session_id, n2.live_solo_screen_state, n2.owner_team_id, n2.payload_expires_at, n2.payload_occurred_at, n2.previous_question_status, n2.previous_state_value, n2.provider_name, n2.question_actor_role, n2.question_status, n2.question_subject, n2.range_from, n2.range_to, n2.reason, n2.related_id, n2.related_type, n2.reply_to_id, n2.runtime_instance_id, n2.runtime_state, n2.sent_at, n2.source_event_key, n2.source_id, n2.source_team_id, n2.source_type, n2.sso_protocol, n2.sso_provider_id, n2.state_value, n2.subject, n2.target_id, n2.target_type, n2.team_id, n2.team_name, n2.thread_root_id, n2.title, n2.user_id, n2.user_lifecycle_action, n2.user_name, n2.value, n3.notification_id, n3.reference_kind, n3.count
FROM (
    SELECT n.id, n.action_value, n.actor_user_id, n.appeal_event_id, n.automatic, n.body, n.challenge_flag_id, n.challenge_title, n.code, n.competition_challenge_id, n.competition_id, n.count, n.direction, n.entry_id, n.gameplay_fact_failure_code, n.gameplay_fact_id, n.gameplay_fact_result, n.gameplay_fact_state, n.hint_id, n.ip_address, n.jwt_id, n.kind, n.live_solo_match_id, n.live_solo_media_alert_kind, n.live_solo_media_session_id, n.live_solo_screen_state, n.owner_team_id, n.payload_expires_at, n.payload_occurred_at, n.previous_question_status, n.previous_state_value, n.provider_name, n.question_actor_role, n.question_status, n.question_subject, n.range_from, n.range_to, n.reason, n.related_id, n.related_type, n.reply_to_id, n.runtime_instance_id, n.runtime_state, n.sent_at, n.source_event_key, n.source_id, n.source_team_id, n.source_type, n.sso_protocol, n.sso_provider_id, n.state_value, n.subject, n.target_id, n.target_type, n.team_id, n.team_name, n.thread_root_id, n.title, n.user_id, n.user_lifecycle_action, n.user_name, n.value
    FROM notifications AS n
    WHERE (n.target_type <> 0 OR n.target_id = @userId) AND (n.kind <> 0 OR n.related_type <> 10 OR n.related_type IS NULL OR n.thread_root_id IS NULL OR n.related_id <> n.thread_root_id OR n.related_id IS NULL OR n.action_value NOT IN (1, 2) OR n.action_value IS NULL OR NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM notifications AS n0
        WHERE n0.id = n.thread_root_id AND n0.kind = 1)) AND (n.kind <> 1 OR NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM notifications AS n1
        WHERE n1.thread_root_id = n.id AND n1.kind = 0 AND n1.related_type = 10 AND n1.related_id = n.id AND n1.action_value = 2)) AND ((n.target_type = 0 AND n.target_id = @userId3) OR (n.target_type = 1 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_CollaboratorCompetitionIds)) OR (n.target_type = 2 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_ParticipantCompetitionIds)) OR (n.target_type = 3 AND n.target_id = ANY (@audience_MemberTeamIds)) OR (n.source_type = 1 AND n.source_id = @userId7) OR (n.thread_root_id IS NULL AND n.id = ANY (@threadRootIds)) OR (n.thread_root_id IS NOT NULL AND n.thread_root_id = ANY (@threadRootIds))) AND n.kind NOT IN (20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25) AND (n.target_type <> 2 OR (n.kind = 1 AND n.source_type = 1)) AND (n.source_type <> 1 OR n.source_id <> @userId10 OR n.source_id IS NULL)
    ORDER BY n.sent_at DESC, n.id DESC
    LIMIT @p12 OFFSET @p
) AS n2
LEFT JOIN notification_reference_counts AS n3 ON n2.id = n3.notification_id
WHERE n2.kind NOT IN (20, 21, 22, 23, 24, 25)
ORDER BY n2.sent_at DESC, n2.id DESC, n3.notification_id
